state-of-the-art machine learning interpretability techniques under one roof. With this package, you can train interpretable glassbox models and explain blackbox systems. InterpretML helps you understand your model's global behavior, or understand the reasons behind individual predictions.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eInterpretability is essential 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eEBM is an interpretable model developed at Microsoft Research\u003csup\u003e\u003ca href=\"#citations\"\u003e*\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/sup\u003e. It uses modern machine learning techniques like bagging, gradient boosting, and automatic interaction detection to breathe new life into traditional GAMs (Generalized Additive Models). This makes EBMs as accurate as state-of-the-art techniques like random forests and gradient boosted trees. However, unlike these blackbox models, EBMs produce exact explanations and are editable by domain <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">In the beginning machines learned in darkness, and data scientists struggled in the void to explain them.</h3><a id="user-content-in-the-beginning-machines-learned-in-darkness-and-data-scientists-struggled-in-the-void-to-explain-them" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: In the beginning machines learned in darkness, and data scientists struggled in the void to explain them." href="#in-the-beginning-machines-learned-in-darkness-and-data-scientists-struggled-in-the-void-to-explain-them"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Let there be light.</h3><a id="user-content-let-there-be-light" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Let there be light." href="#let-there-be-light"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
</blockquote>
<p dir="auto">InterpretML is an open-source package that incorporates state-of-the-art machine learning interpretability techniques under one roof. With this package, you can train interpretable glassbox models and explain blackbox systems. InterpretML helps you understand your model's global behavior, or understand the reasons behind individual predictions.</p>
<p dir="auto">Interpretability is essential for:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Model debugging - Why did my model make this mistake?</li>
<li>Feature Engineering - How can I improve my model?</li>
<li>Detecting fairness issues - Does my model discriminate?</li>
<li>Human-AI cooperation - How can I understand and trust the model's decisions?</li>
<li>Regulatory compliance - Does my model satisfy legal requirements?</li>
<li>High-risk applications - Healthcare, finance, judicial, ...</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="https://github.com/interpretml/interpretml.github.io/blob/master/interpret-highlight.gif"><img src="https://github.com/interpretml/interpretml.github.io/raw/master/interpret-highlight.gif" alt="" data-animated-image="" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installation</h1><a id="user-content-installation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installation" href="#installation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Python 3.10+ | Linux, Mac, Windows</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install interpret
# OR
conda install -c conda-forge interpret"><pre>pip install interpret
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> OR</span>
conda install -c conda-forge interpret</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Introducing the Explainable Boosting Machine (EBM)</h1><a id="user-content-introducing-the-explainable-boosting-machine-ebm" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Introducing the Explainable Boosting Machine (EBM)" href="#introducing-the-explainable-boosting-machine-ebm"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">EBM is an interpretable model developed at Microsoft Research<sup><a href="#citations">*</a></sup>. It uses modern machine learning techniques like bagging, gradient boosting, and automatic interaction detection to breathe new life into traditional GAMs (Generalized Additive Models). This makes EBMs as accurate as state-of-the-art techniques like random forests and gradient boosted trees. However, unlike these blackbox models, EBMs produce exact explanations and are editable by domain experts.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Train a glassbox model</h1><a id="user-content-train-a-glassbox-model" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Train a glassbox model" href="#train-a-glassbox-model"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Let's fit an Explainable Boosting Machine</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from interpret.glassbox import ExplainableBoostingClassifier

ebm = ExplainableBoostingClassifier()
ebm.fit(X_train, y_train)

# or substitute with LogisticRegression, DecisionTreeClassifier, RuleListClassifier, ...
# EBM supports pandas dataframes, numpy arrays, and handles &quot;string&quot; data natively."><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">interpret</span>.<span class="pl-s1">glassbox</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">ExplainableBoostingClassifier</span>

<span class="pl-s1">ebm</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">ExplainableBoostingClassifier</span>()
<span class="pl-s1">ebm</span>.<span class="pl-c1">fit</span>(<span class="pl-v">X_train</span>, <span class="pl-s1">y_train</span>)

<span class="pl-c"># or substitute with LogisticRegression, DecisionTreeClassifier, RuleListClassifier, ...</span>
<span class="pl-c"># EBM supports pandas dataframes, numpy arrays, and handles "string" data natively.</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">Understand the model</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from interpret import show

ebm_global = ebm.explain_global()
show(ebm_global)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">interpret</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-s1">show</span>

<span class="pl-s1">ebm_global</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">ebm</span>.<span class="pl-c1">explain_global</span>()
<span class="pl-en">show</span>(<span class="pl-s1">ebm_global</span>)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/interpretml/interpret/blob/main/docs/readme/ebm-global.png?raw=true"><img src="/interpretml/interpret/raw/main/docs/readme/ebm-global.png?raw=true" alt="Global Explanation Image" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<br>
<p dir="auto">Understand individual predictions</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="ebm_local = ebm.explain_local(X_test, y_test)
show(ebm_local)"><pre><span class="pl-s1">ebm_local</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">ebm</span>.<span class="pl-c1">explain_local</span>(<span class="pl-v">X_test</span>, <span class="pl-s1">y_test</span>)
<span class="pl-en">show</span>(<span class="pl-s1">ebm_local</span>)</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/interpretml/interpret/blob/main/docs/readme/ebm-local.png?raw=true"><img src="/interpretml/interpret/raw/main/docs/readme/ebm-local.png?raw=true" alt="Local Explanation Image" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<br>
<p dir="auto">And if you have multiple model explanations, compare them</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="show([logistic_regression_global, decision_tree_global])"><pre><span class="pl-en">show</span>([<span class="pl-s1">logistic_regression_global</span>, <span class="pl-s1">decision_tree_global</span>])</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/interpretml/interpret/blob/main/docs/readme/dashboard.png?raw=true"><img src="/interpretml/interpret/raw/main/docs/readme/dashboard.png?raw=true" alt="Dashboard Image" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<br>
<p dir="auto">If you need to keep your data private, use Differentially Private EBMs (see <a href="https://proceedings.mlr.press/v139/nori21a/nori21a.pdf" rel="nofollow">DP-EBMs</a>)</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from interpret.privacy import (
    DPExplainableBoostingClassifier,
    DPExplainableBoostingRegressor,
)

dp_ebm = DPExplainableBoostingClassifier(
    epsilon=1, delta=1e-5
)  # Specify privacy parameters
dp_ebm.fit(X_train, y_train)

show(dp_ebm.explain_global())  # Identical function calls to standard EBMs"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">interpret</span>.<span class="pl-s1">privacy</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> (
    <span class="pl-v">DPExplainableBoostingClassifier</span>,
    <span class="pl-v">DPExplainableBoostingRegressor</span>,
)

<span class="pl-s1">dp_ebm</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">DPExplainableBoostingClassifier</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">epsilon</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">1</span>, <span class="pl-s1">delta</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">1e-5</span>
)  <span class="pl-c"># Specify privacy parameters</span>
<span class="pl-s1">dp_ebm</span>.<span class="pl-c1">fit</span>(<span class="pl-v">X_train</span>, <span class="pl-s1">y_train</span>)

<span class="pl-en">show</span>(<span class="pl-s1">dp_ebm</span>.<span class="pl-c1">explain_global</span>())  <span class="pl-c"># Identical function calls to standard EBMs</span></pre></div>
<br>
<br>
<p dir="auto">For more information, see the <a href="https://interpret.ml/docs" rel="nofollow">documentation</a>.</p>
<br>
<p dir="auto">EBMs include pairwise interactions by default. For 3-way interactions and higher see this notebook: <a href="https://interpret.ml/docs/python/examples/custom-interactions.html" rel="nofollow">https://interpret.ml/docs/python/examples/custom-interactions.html</a></p>
<br>
<p dir="auto">Interpret EBMs can be fit on datasets with 100 million samples in several hours. For larger workloads consider using distributed EBMs on Azure SynapseML: <a href="https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/fabric/data-science/explainable-boosting-machines-classification" rel="nofollow">classification EBMs</a> and <a href="https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/fabric/data-science/explainable-boosting-machines-regression" rel="nofollow">regression EBMs</a></p>
<br>
